This modular design provides a scalable approach to identity generation while remaining compatible with existing and future identity-conditioned DMs. Experiments indicate that IDPrior generates diverse and well-separated identities while maintaining a closer correspondence to the distribution of authentic identities. Face recognition models trained on datasets generated using IDPrior-generated identities achieve competitive or improved performance compared with existing synthetic-data-based methods across multiple face verification benchmarks. Code and generated datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/fdbtrs/IDperturb.

</details>


### [75] [Rotation Is Token-Blind: A Structural Criterion for Post-Training Quantization of Feed-Forward Geometry Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07955)
*Huanfei He,Bohao Zhang,Jiaxin Zhang,Wei Sui,John See,Zeyd Boukhers,Cong Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: QMapAnything 是面向前馈式三维重建模型的结构感知训练后量化框架，针对不同令牌类型的量化退化差异，在保持精度的同时降低部署开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长序列和深层注意力导致计算及内存成本高；现有逐通道平滑或旋转方法忽略异构令牌共享量化网格的问题，尤其是对全局输出影响较大的特殊令牌。

Method: 将保持浮点计算不变的旋转与平滑建模为令牌无关重参数化群，分析其对不同令牌信噪比的影响；提出结合特殊令牌量化劣势和输出影响力的统计量 Γ，用于判断是否需要额外的差异化偏差校正。

Result: 几何基准测试中，W4A8 性能接近全精度；W8A8 相比 FP32 的峰值内存降低 2.2–3.1 倍，真实硬件上最高加速 3.1 倍。

Conclusion: 考虑令牌结构及其输出影响力的训练后量化，有助于在资源受限的边缘设备上高效部署前馈式三维重建模型。

Abstract: Feed-forward 3D models use Transformers to predict camera poses, depth, and structure in one pass, avoiding iterative multi-view optimization. Long sequences and deep attention incur high compute and memory costs, motivating Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) for deployment. Existing PTQ methods mainly suppress channel-wise outliers by smoothing or orthogonal rotation. Geometry Transformers introduce a different challenge: heterogeneous token types share one activation quantization grid. A few special tokens may exert high leverage over global outputs and be updated by only a subset of blocks. Token-blind transforms, which apply the same transformation to every token, reduce overall quantization error but cannot remove differentiated degradation across token types. We propose QMapAnything, a structure-aware PTQ framework. We cast floating-point-preserving rotation and smoothing as a token-blind reparameterization group, and prove that they yield common-mode gains while leaving relative token SNR unchanged. We introduce a statistic $Γ$ that combines a special token's quantization disadvantage and output leverage to predict whether differentiated bias correction is needed beyond rotation. Experiments on geometric benchmarks show that W4A8 closely matches full-precision performance. Under W8A8, peak memory falls by $2.2$--$3.1\times$ versus FP32, with up to $3.1\times$ speedup on real hardware. These results provide a principled and efficient approach to deploying feed-forward 3D reconstruction models in resource-constrained edges.

</details>


### [76] [ProQuant: Progressive Quantization-aware Training for Edge MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08216)
*Yufei Xue,Yushi Huang,Jiawei Shao,Pingcheng Dong,Yonghao Tan,Shiyao Li,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Jun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProQuant 是面向低比特多模态大语言模型的量化感知训练框架，通过自重标注和渐进式模块激活降低量化性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型计算和内存开销较高，限制了边缘部署；训练后量化在 W4A8 等低比特设置下性能显著下降，小规模模型尤其明显。

Method: 采用权重—激活量化感知训练：通过自重标注重新采样开源多模态数据集的回答，以缓解微调与预训练数据分布的差异；将多模态理解与语言建模能力解耦，利用渐进式模块激活优先恢复多模态理解，并通过视觉敏感因子识别多模态关键层。

Result: 在 2B–8B 参数模型及多个基准上的实验中，ProQuant 优于现有方法。W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct 的平均准确率提高 6.6%，相对全精度模型的性能下降约 1%；摘要未明确说明 6.6% 的比较基线或其是相对百分比还是百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，结合数据重标注与针对多模态能力的渐进式量化训练，有望使低比特模型接近全精度性能，促进资源受限设备上的部署。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) with impressive perception and understanding capabilities will enable a range of downstream applications. However, their substantial computational and memory requirements hinder the real-world implementation, particularly for resource-constrained edge devices. Quantization has emerged as an effective solution for deploying large models. While post-training quantization (PTQ) successfully retains the performance at moderate bit-width (e.g., W8A8), it suffers from severe performance degradation under low-bit quantization (e.g., W4A8), especially for small-scale models that are more suitable for edge deployment. In this paper, we propose ProQuant, a novel weight-activation quantization-aware training (QAT) framework tailored for low-bit MLLMs. First, to align the limited finetuning dataset distribution with the pretrained dataset distribution, we propose a self-relabeling (SRL) scheme to resample the responses of the open-source multimodal dataset. Second, we are the first to take a decoupled view of multi-modal (MM) understanding and language modeling abilities. We propose a progressive block activation (PBA) mechanism to prioritize the MM understanding recovery. We also introduce a vision-sensitive factor $\vecτ$ that effectively identifies the MM-critical layers. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks, covering models with 2B-8B parameters, show that ProQuant significantly outperforms existing methods. For example, our W4A8 Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct improves average accuracy by impressively 6.6% and achieves performance comparable to full precision counterparts with only approximately 1% performance drop. Code is available at https://github.com/yufeixue-ai/ProQuant/.

</details>


### [77] [Bri3D: Bridging the Object-Scene Gap in Single-Image 3D Generation via Scene-Anchor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07961)
*Hongye Hou,Shuai Zhang,Hongjie Li,Zhuoxiao Li,Haiping Wang,Yuan Liu,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Bri3D利用实例感知体素场景锚点连接场景布局推理与预训练物体生成先验，从单张图像高效生成布局一致、细节丰富的3D场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像组合式3D场景生成需要同时恢复物体几何与空间关系；场景级监督有限，直接扩展物体生成器容易损害几何质量，并丢失实例的尺度、朝向和姿态。

Method: 先预测以颜色区分实例的场景脚手架，编码粗略几何和空间布局；再提取并细化各实例锚点，形成保持姿态且兼容物体先验的体素支撑；最后使用冻结的预训练物体生成器，在锚点定义的空间参考中生成带纹理的精细几何。

Result: 摘要报告场景布局、物体几何、视觉质量和泛化能力均有提升；场景布局预测高效，完整场景生成速度快于SceneGen和SAM3D，但未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 将场景组织与物体细节合成解耦，并复用预训练物体先验，有助于兼顾空间一致性、生成质量和效率；具体提升幅度仍需查阅全文。

Abstract: Generating compositional 3D scenes from a single image requires recovering detailed object geometry and coherent spatial arrangements. Existing object-to-scene methods, including SAM3D and MIDI, extend object generation through image-context reasoning or inter-instance modeling. Coordinating objects remains challenging with limited scene-level supervision. Directly extending object-centric generators to entire scenes can degrade geometry and fail to preserve instance scale, orientation, and pose. We propose Bri3D, a framework bridging scene-level structural reasoning and object-level generative priors through instance-aware voxel scene anchors. Bri3D predicts a color-coded scene scaffold encoding coarse object geometry and spatial arrangements. Each instance is extracted as a scene anchor and refined into a pose-preserving, prior-compatible voxel support, enabling a frozen object generator to synthesize detailed textured geometry in the anchor-defined spatial reference. This coarse-to-fine design separates scene organization from object-scale detail synthesis, focusing scene-level training on spatial organization and anchor refinement while reusing pretrained object priors. Experiments demonstrate improvements in scene layout, object geometry, visual quality, and generalization. Bri3D predicts scene layouts efficiently; full scene generation is faster than SceneGen and SAM3D.

</details>


### [78] [Reviving the Discarded High-Resolution Feature for Transformer-Based Tiny Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07994)
*Zehao Wang,Si Wu,Guiping Cao,Xin Li,Yaowei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReDHF通过融合、监督和查询交互重新利用高分辨率特征，提升DETR类检测器的微小目标检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微小目标像素少，细节在下采样中易丢失；最高分辨率特征虽保留细节，但计算成本高，直接引入还会增加背景噪声。

Method: 提出三个协作模块：CSDF跨尺度可变形融合，增强最高分辨率特征的语义并向浅层特征注入细节；EGSS采用几何自适应辅助监督，强化分类和定位先验；FSQI使解码器查询同时利用低层细节和高层语义。

Result: 最佳变体相较各自基线在AI-TOD-v2、VisDrone和SODA-D上分别提升1.4、1.6和1.5 AP，对微小及小目标的增益尤为明显；摘要报告其在所评测基准上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 有针对性地恢复和利用高分辨率特征，可以改善不同DETR变体的微小目标检测；具体计算开销和实验设置未在摘要中给出。

Abstract: Tiny object detection remains challenging because tiny instances occupy only a few pixels, and their fine-grained details are easily lost during feature downsampling. Although the highest-resolution feature preserves rich local details for tiny objects, DETR-like detectors often discard it due to its large computational cost, while directly reintroducing this feature also brings dense background noise. To address this problem, we propose ReDHF, a novel framework that Revives the Discarded High-resolution Feature for tiny object detection. ReDHF has three cooperative components. Cross-Scale Deformable Fusion (CSDF) first constructs a semantically enhanced highest-resolution feature and injects its fine-grained details into the shallow feature under a moderate cost. Ellipse-Guided Shallow Supervision (EGSS) then guides the enhanced feature to learn more reliable classification and localization priors by applying geometry-adaptive auxiliary supervision. Full-Scale Query Interaction (FSQI) further enables decoder queries to access both low-level details and high-level semantic information, allowing them to aggregate richer cues for tiny objects. Extensive experiments show that ReDHF consistently improves multiple baselines across different DETR variants and achieves state-of-the-art performance on the evaluated benchmarks. The best ReDHF variants surpass their baselines by +1.4 AP on AI-TOD-v2, +1.6 AP on VisDrone, and +1.5 AP on SODA-D, with especially notable gains on tiny and small objects.

</details>


### [79] [When GeoFMs Meet VHR: Benchmarking Foundation Models on Very High Resolution Earth Observation Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08003)
*Kshitij Raj Sharma,Pierre Adorni,Minh-Tan Pham,Dirk Tiede,Sébastien Lefèvre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对超高分辨率遥感任务，通用自然图像预训练与领域预训练各有优势；在强通用模型上继续进行领域自监督预训练可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型多基于10米或更粗分辨率影像预训练，其向能够分辨建筑和道路的超高分辨率影像任务迁移的效果尚需系统评估。

Method: 构建VHR-Bench，涵盖10个地面采样距离不大于1.5米的数据集、5类任务和8个应用领域；在冻结骨干网络及两种标注数据比例下评估三个预训练层级的12个编码器，并使用102万个独立VHR图块继续自监督预训练DINOv3 ViT-L。

Result: 标注稀缺时SkySense领先；使用全部标注时，网页图像预训练的DINOv3 ViT-L略胜，两者表现接近。仅在Sentinel尺度影像上预训练的编码器在RGB输入下迁移表现最弱。继续预训练后的DINOv3在少标注时明显改善、全标注时小幅改善，推理参数量和计算量不变。

Conclusion: 结果支持先通用化、后专门化的策略：强大的自然图像预训练模型经短期VHR领域继续预训练后，可进一步提升遥感任务表现，同时保留通用视觉特征。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models are a widely adopted starting point for remote sensing tasks. Most are pretrained on Sentinel-1/2 and Landsat imagery at a ground sampling distance (GSD) of 10m or coarser. Many applications instead rely on very high resolution (VHR) imagery, where individual buildings, roads, and amenities are resolved. We study which pretraining regimes transfer well to this scale. We introduce VHR-Bench, a benchmark suite of ten datasets at 1.5m GSD or finer spanning five task families, eight application domains, and both satellite and aerial platforms. We evaluate twelve encoders in three pretraining tiers with frozen backbones at two training-data fractions. We find that both pretraining on large natural-image collections and VHR-specific pretraining transfer effectively to VHR tasks, with complementary strengths. SkySense leads when labels are scarce; at the full label fraction the web-pretrained DINOv3 ViT-L edges ahead, with the two close together there. Encoders pretrained only on Sentinel-scale imagery transfer least well under RGB input. Guided by these complementary results, we continue the self-supervised pretraining of DINOv3 ViT-L on 1.02 million held-out VHR tiles and evaluate the resulting encoder without changing the downstream protocol. The continued model gives a clear improvement over the released backbone when labels are scarce and a modest one at the full fraction, at no increase in inference parameters or compute. These findings support a generalize-then-specialize strategy: already strong, broad natural-image pretraining can be further improved by a short VHR continuation stage while preserving its general visual features.

</details>


### [80] [Deformable Contact-Aware 3D Object Placement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08005)
*Songhe Wang,David Miller,Charles A. Kamhoua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DCAP 将语言引导的三维物体放置建模为受重力和摩擦作用的释放至平衡过程，以改善物理真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法直接预测刚体六自由度位姿，容易出现悬浮、支撑面穿透，并忽略软表面变形。

Method: 先根据语义意图构造包含释放位置、物体尺度和可变形组件的释放状态，再通过刚体—可变形体耦合模拟器求解平衡状态，从而区分语义规划与物理实现。

Result: 在包含 2,063 个提示词—资产—场景案例的基准上，相比最强原始刚体基线，标准放置任务的 VLM 物理性与自然度得分提高 21.2 点，人工偏好提高 26.5 点，平均穿透深度降低 72.3%；相比模拟展开基线，残余速度降低 72.1%。

Conclusion: 实验支持结合语义释放状态与物理模拟改善放置效果，但摘要中的结果不足以证明物理准确的放置必须严格分离语义意图与物理实现。

Abstract: Current language-guided 3D object placement typically predicts a rigid 6-DoF pose, ignoring the physical reality that indoor placements are dictated by contact. Consequently, predicted poses often float, interpenetrate supports, or fail to deform soft surfaces. We propose Deformable Contact-Aware Placement (DCAP), a framework that reframes placement as a drop-to-equilibrium process. Rather than predicting a static pose, DCAP constructs a semantic release state that specifies the drop location, object scale, and deformable components, allowing a coupled rigid-deformable simulator to settle the object under gravity and friction. This explicitly separates semantic intent from physical realization. Evaluated on a benchmark of 2,063 prompt-asset-scene cases, DCAP significantly outperforms static baselines. On standard placements, it improves VLM Physics & Naturalness by 21.2 points and human preference by 26.5 points over the strongest raw rigid baseline, while reducing mean penetration depth by 72.3%. Compared to simulated rollout baselines, DCAP reduces residual speed by 72.1%. Ultimately, our results prove that physically accurate 3D placement requires modeling semantic intent and physical realization as strictly separate processes.

</details>


### [81] [NutriVisRAG: Multimodal Exemplar Grounding for Nutrient Estimation with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08054)
*Lubnaa Abdur Rahman,Ioannis Papathanail,Maria Panagiotou,Nefeli Dimitropoulou,Margret Harðardóttir,Elisabeth J. den Brok,Bastiaan E. de Galan,Stavroula Georgia Mougiakakou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: NutriVisRAG通过检索视觉相似且带营养标注的餐食作为多模态示例，无需任务微调即可改善部分配置下的营养估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实生活场景中的餐食图像难以准确用于膳食评估；现有检索增强视觉语言模型方法主要依赖文本食物表示，尚未充分探索直接利用视觉相似的标注餐食。

Method: 使用视觉编码器检索具有已知营养标注的相似餐食，将图像及其标注作为上下文示例，在推理时辅助视觉语言模型估计营养成分，无需针对任务微调。

Result: 在4个数据集、4个视觉语言模型、3种检索编码器和2种提示策略组成的96种配置中，56种的主要估计误差降低；整餐提示为35/48，逐项提示为21/48。16个模型—数据集组合中，13个的NutriVisRAG最低误差低于最佳Vanilla基线。GPT-5.4-mini采用整餐提示和各数据集最佳检索编码器时，四个数据集的主要误差均降低，降幅为1.7%—35.7%。

Conclusion: 以图像及其营养标注组成的多模态示例为依据，可以在不进行任务微调的情况下提升营养估计表现，但收益取决于模型、数据集、检索编码器和提示策略等配置。

Abstract: Diet is a major determinant of health, yet accurate dietary assessment from free-living meal images remains challenging. Existing retrieval-based Vision-Language Model (VLM) approaches to nutrient estimation typically rely on textual food representations, while, to our knowledge, direct grounding on visually similar annotated meals has not been investigated. We present NutriVisRAG, a retrieval-augmented generation framework that uses a vision encoder to retrieve similar meals with known nutritional annotations and provide them as multimodal in-context exemplars for VLM nutrient estimation at inference time, without task-specific fine-tuning. We evaluate NutriVisRAG across four datasets, four VLMs, three retrieval encoders, and two prompting strategies, including free-living meals from adults with insulin-treated diabetes, where carbohydrate estimates are compared against two dietitians' annotations. Across all 96 configurations (4 datasets x 4 VLMs x 3 retrieval encoders x 2 prompting strategies), NutriVisRAG reduced the primary estimation error in 56, with improvements more frequent under whole-meal (35/48) than per-item prompting (21/48). Across the evaluated configurations, the lowest NutriVisRAG error was below the best Vanilla error in 13 of 16 VLM--dataset pairs. For GPT-5.4-mini, under whole-meal prompting, the best-performing retrieval encoder for each dataset reduced the primary error on all four datasets relative to Vanilla, with reductions of 1.7--35.7%. Overall, our results show that grounding in multimodal exemplars comprising images and respective annotations can improve nutrient estimation at inference time without task-specific fine-tuning, although the benefits remain configuration-dependent.

</details>


### [82] [Spatiotemporal Dynamics-Aware Industrial Anomaly Detection and Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08080)
*S-Wei Chen,Siangyu Yang,Yi-Chen Jan,Chun-Yi Lee,Shao-Yuan Lo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MUSTARD利用光流引导的关键帧选择与多模态增强，使多模态大语言模型仅凭少量正常样本即可检测并解释工业动态异常，无需模型更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业异常检测主要依赖静态视觉特征，难以识别异常运动；现有多模态大语言模型的时序建模能力也存在不足。

Method: 通过光流引导选择关键帧，并引入增强模态，结合局部运动模式与全局动态演化；基于少量正常参考样本进行异常推理，无需更新模型。

Result: 摘要称实验显示，该方法在时空异常检测及生成合理解释方面表现优越，但未提供具体数据、基线或评测指标。

Conclusion: MUSTARD为动态工业异常检测提供了可跨产品高效适配、决策可解释的方案；作者称其为首个基于多模态大语言模型的动态工业异常检测框架。

Abstract: Industrial Anomaly Detection (IAD) is a long-established problem that aims to detect defects in industrial products during manufacturing for quality control. Most existing studies focus on static visual patterns as the basis for detection. However, in real manufacturing environments, many anomalies are dynamics-based, such as irregular movements that cannot be perceived from static cues alone. We observe that even recent Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM)-based approaches fall short in identifying such dynamic anomalies due to limited temporal modeling capability. To address this, we introduce MUSTARD: MUltimodal SpatioTemporal Anomaly Reasoning and Detection, a framework that equips MLLMs with fine-grained spatiotemporal dynamics awareness for detecting and reasoning about dynamics-based defects. MUSTARD introduces optical-flow-guided keyframe selection and augmented modalities to capture critical motion irregularities through local motion patterns and global dynamic evolution. Its normal pattern-based reasoning pipeline requires only a few normal reference samples as the detection basis, avoiding any model updates. This enables efficient adaptation across various products and provides high decision interpretability. MUSTARD is the first MLLM-based framework for dynamics-based IAD. Experiments demonstrate its superior performance in detecting spatiotemporal anomalies and providing plausible explanations. The code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [83] [TerraMesh-Masks: Open-Vocabulary Segmentation for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08081)
*Benedikt Blumenstiel,Hugues Devimeux,Johannes Jakubik,Konrad Schindler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出面向地球观测的开放词汇分割数据集 TerraMesh-Masks，并构建开放词汇版本的 TerraMind，显著优于通用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然图像开放词汇分割方法因语义、尺度、传感模态和地理背景差异，难以迁移至地球观测数据；该领域也缺乏大规模专用数据集和基准。

Method: 构建包含1600万个二值掩膜、与900万个多模态 TerraMesh 样本对齐的数据集，使用 OpenStreetMap 和 Overture 衍生语义进行标注；另设523个经专家审查、覆盖200多个类别的评估样本，并构建开放词汇版本的 TerraMind。

Result: 所构建模型的 IoU 达35.18%，高于 DINOv3.txt 的16.29%和 SAM-3 的11.33%。

Conclusion: 地球观测专用数据有助于提升开放词汇分割性能；数据集已以宽松许可证公开。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary segmentation (OVS) enables models to segment a variety of concepts specified in natural language, removing the need for fixed label sets and fine-tuning. While recent OVS approaches have shown strong performance on natural images, they transfer poorly to Earth observation (EO) data due to differing semantics, scales, sensing modality, and geographic context. To date, large-scale EO-specific datasets and benchmarks for OVS remain scarce. We, therefore, introduce TerraMesh-Masks, a large-scale open-vocabulary segmentation dataset for EO, comprising 16 million binary masks aligned with nine million multimodal TerraMesh samples and annotated using OpenStreetMap and Overture-derived semantics. In addition, we provide an expert-reviewed evaluation set of 523 samples covering over 200 classes to enable robust evaluation. To demonstrate the impact of TerraMesh-Masks, we build an open-vocabulary version of TerraMind. Our results demonstrate significantly improved performance over general-purpose state-of-the-art models reaching 35.18% IoU versus 16.29% for DINOv3.txt and 11.33% for SAM-3. We release the dataset publicly under a permissive license at https://huggingface.co/datasets/ibm-esa-geospatial/TerraMesh-Masks.

</details>


### [84] [National-scale mapping of tree species composition in Switzerland from high-resolution aerial imagery and multimodal data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08095)
*Yixin Zhou,Luca Ferrari,Verena C. Griess,Nataliia Rehush,Konrad Schindler,Mirela Beloiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究利用多源遥感和深度学习，绘制瑞士30米分辨率的树种胸高断面积占比地图，揭示传统优势树种地图遗漏的混交信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树种组成影响森林生产力及其对干旱、风倒和虫害的响应；现有单一优势树种地图无法反映伴生树种的占比。

Method: 融合0.1米航空RGB影像、机载激光扫描、年度AlphaEarth卫星嵌入及气候变量，以3,992个瑞士国家森林清查样地的胸高断面积观测训练深度学习模型，预测10种主要树种及其他类别的占比。

Result: 11类空间交叉验证宏平均R²为0.485；独立森林保护区数据上，像元和样地宏平均R²分别为0.552和0.733，优势类别识别准确率分别为73.2%和81.1%。地图还识别出广泛分布的少量枫树和白蜡树，并再现树种随海拔变化的序列。

Conclusion: 空间连续的树种混合比例地图可补充森林清查统计，支持森林监测、混交林管理与气候适应规划；其泛化表现仍需结合不同地区和条件进一步验证。

Abstract: Tree-species composition shapes forest productivity and how forests respond to climate disturbances such as drought, windthrow and insect outbreaks. Yet most national forest maps assign each pixel a single dominant species and cannot show the share of accompanying species. We mapped the basal-area shares of ten main tree species and a residual Other class across Switzerland at 30 m resolution. A deep-learning model combining 0.1 m aerial RGB imagery, airborne laser scanning (ALS), annual AlphaEarth satellite embeddings, and climate variables was trained on basal-area observations from 3,992 Swiss National Forest Inventory (NFI) sites. In spatial cross-validation, the model reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.485 across the 11 classes. On an independent forest-reserve dataset, the map reached a macro-$R^2$ of 0.552 for individual 30 m pixels and 0.733 for plots, and identified the dominant class in 73.2% of pixels and 81.1% of plots. The map shows maple and ash as widespread minor components that dominant-class maps omit. It also reproduces the elevational sequence from broadleaved lowland stands through spruce to larch and Arolla pine near the treeline. By resolving the species mixture within each 30 m pixel, the map complements field inventory statistics with spatially continuous information that can support forest monitoring, the management of mixed stands, and climate-adaptation planning across Switzerland.

</details>


### [85] [Towards Practical 20 kbps Generative Video Compression via Adaptive Tokenization and Entropy Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08099)
*Jiancheng Zhao,Jixiang Luo,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 将预训练视频表征适配为面向压缩的离散词元，并联合优化分词器与熵模型，提升超低码率下的感知压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低码率易丢失重要感知细节；现有方法多在重建阶段利用生成先验，且生成与重建表征未针对熵编码优化。

Method: 将 MiniMax-H3 的预训练因果视频 VAE 适配为离散视频分词器；采用轻量自回归熵模型捕获局部和全局词元依赖，并通过分组预测实现并行解码；利用可微码率代理联合优化分词器和熵模型。

Result: 在 UVG、MCL-JCV 和 HEVC Class B 上，相较 GLC-Video，BD-LPIPS 节省 82.2%–87.5%，BD-DISTS 节省 78.3%–85.0%。

Conclusion: 面向熵编码适配预训练视频表征，并联合优化码率与重建，可改善超低码率视频压缩的感知率失真性能。

Abstract: Video compression at ultra-low bitrates remains challenging due to the loss of perceptually important details under limited bit budgets. Recent generative video compression methods leverage pretrained video priors to improve perceptual quality and temporal coherence, but many follow a compress-then-generate paradigm, where generative priors are primarily exploited during reconstruction. Moreover, representations learned for generation and reconstruction are not inherently optimized for entropy coding.
  In this work, we propose \method, which adapts pretrained video representations into compression-oriented representations while preserving their reconstruction capability. Specifically, we adapt the pretrained causal video VAE of MiniMax-H3 into a discrete video tokenizer and develop a small autoregressive entropy model that captures local and global token dependencies, with grouped prediction for parallel decoding. To further improve coding efficiency, we jointly optimize the tokenizer and entropy model using a differentiable rate surrogate, allowing rate gradients to adapt token assignments toward lower coding costs while preserving reconstruction quality. Experiments on UVG, MCL-JCV, and HEVC Class B demonstrate superior perceptual rate--distortion performance, achieving BD-LPIPS savings of 82.2\%--87.5\% and BD-DISTS savings of 78.3\%--85.0\% relative to GLC-Video.

</details>


### [86] [ProtoGestalt: Interpretable Prototypes for Facial Phenotyping and Diagnosis of Rare Genetic Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08125)
*Ranga Baminiwatte,Alexander Hustinx,Fabio Hellmann,Aaron J Masino*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProtoGestalt 是一种可解释的面部表型分析框架，用于罕见遗传病筛查和综合征识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 罕见遗传病种类繁多且临床特征重叠，诊断困难；现有面部诊断模型的可解释性及对未确诊疾病广泛筛查的支持有限。

Method: 将原型学习与面部区域约束结合，使模型基于特定解剖区域的局部、可加证据作出预测；在多个对照数据集及不同类别患病比例下评估筛查能力，并评估对训练期间见过的综合征的识别能力。

Result: 学习到的原型表征可显著区分综合征患者与病例匹配的对照图像；随着对照样本比例增加，模型仍保持一定的有效性能，并能区分不同综合征。摘要未提供具体性能指标。

Conclusion: ProtoGestalt 为罕见病面部分类提供了可解释的替代方案，可能辅助临床决策和表型—基因关联发现；其临床应用价值仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Rare genetic disorders are difficult to diagnose due to their diversity and overlapping clinical features. Facial morphology can provide diagnostic clues, yet most diagnostic models offer limited interpretability and are not designed to support broad screening for undiagnosed disease. We present ProtoGestalt, an interpretable prototype-based framework for facial phenotyping, rare-disease screening, and syndrome identification. The method extends prototype learning by constraining learned visual features to select facial regions, allowing predictions to be explained through localized and additive evidence. We assess ProtoGestalt's ability to distinguish syndromic from unaffected faces on multiple control datasets and class prevalence values. We further evaluate it's diagnostic performance on syndromes observed during training. The learned prototype-based representations provide significant separation between syndromic and case-matched control images and retain useful performance as the proportion of controls increases. The framework also demonstrates the ability to differentiate among syndromes. Although reasonably accurate specialized models for facial classification of rare disorders already exist, ProtoGestalt offers an interpretable alternative that links predictions to anatomically meaningful facial regions, potentially supporting clinical decision-making and the discovery of phenotype-gene associations.

</details>


### [87] [FreeOcc: Decoupling Ego-Motion for Efficient 4D Occupancy Forecasting via Continuous Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08128)
*Zeping Zhang,Zhuoya Zhao,Samy Metari,Robert Laganière*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FreeOcc 利用显式自运动解耦和不确定性感知计算，实现轻量、高效且鲁棒的 4D 占用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型依赖复杂交叉注意力或深层递归来区分自车运动与场景动态，计算开销大，而下游规划器已提供自车未来运动信息。

Method: 在条件流匹配框架中引入 SE(2) 几何解耦，消除自运动影响；采用无注意力时序融合及受物理约束的 ODE 建模残余运动，并根据体素不确定性选择单次解码或 ODE 求解。

Result: 模型参数量为 28.3M，约为可比模型的 40%；集成模式达到 27 FPS。nuScenes 上 NFCD 为 0.47 m、mIoU 为 32.1%；可零样本迁移至 SemanticKITTI，且在严重丢包和时间戳抖动下保留理想 mIoU 的 92.8%。

Conclusion: 显式利用规划轨迹并按不确定性分配计算，有望兼顾占用预测的精度、效率和鲁棒性；具体实验设置与对比细节需查阅论文全文。

Abstract: Current 4D occupancy world models use heavy cross-attention or deep recurrence to disentangle ego-motion from scene dynamics. This requires significant computational capacity. However, this effort is largely redundant since the ego-vehicle's intended future motion is provided by the downstream motion planner. We present FreeOcc, a lightweight continuous-time world model that introduces explicit $SE(2)$ geometric decoupling into a conditional flow-matching framework. By mathematically factoring out self-motion, we create a purely dynamic feature space. This space supports attention-free temporal fusion and allows physics-constrained ODEs to operate strictly on residual agent kinematics. To maximize deployment efficiency, FreeOcc introduces an uncertainty-aware blending mechanism. This routes well-observed voxels to a single-pass decoder, reserving the ODE solver exclusively for ambiguous, high-uncertainty regions. Requiring only 28.3M parameters-roughly 40% of comparable models-our optimal ensemble mode (FreeOcc-E) runs at 27 FPS. On nuScenes, FreeOcc achieves state-of-the-art forecasting performance with a 0.47 m Near Field Chamfer Distance (NFCD) and 32.1% mIoU. Furthermore, the model transfers zero-shot to SemanticKITTI. It also proves highly robust, retaining 92.8% of its ideal mIoU under significant sensor packet loss and timestamp jitter.

</details>


### [88] [Describe Your Facial Expressions by Linking Image Encoders and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08138)
*Yujian Yuan,Jiabei Zeng,Shiguang Shan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出面部表情描述任务，利用合成图文数据和三阶段视觉语言训练，生成兼顾面部动作与情绪的自然语言描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统情绪类别和面部动作单元（AU）难以充分表达表情细节；同时，该任务缺乏配对的训练图文数据。

Method: 利用GPT-3.5与现有情绪类别或AU标注合成、组合描述，构建图文对；结合BLIP-2预训练技术，采用三阶段框架训练视觉语言模型。

Result: 模型能够描述面部动作、情绪及二者之间的推断关系，并可描述训练数据中未出现的情绪；中间视觉特征在AU检测和情绪分类任务上的表现也得到提升。

Conclusion: 合成描述与视觉语言训练可缓解标注数据不足，支持细粒度表情描述，并提升相关视觉任务的特征表现。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel task of describing human facial expressions of a facial image in natural language, which captures the nuances of facial actions and emotional states beyond traditional emotion categories or facial action units (AUs). To achieve the facial expression captioning model, we propose a three-stage training framework that trains a vision-to-language model using synthesized image-text pairs and the BLIP-2 pre-training techniques. To overcome the challenge of missing training image-text pairs for facial expression captioning, we propose a strategy that involves synthesizing and combining captions using GPT-3.5 and existing annotations on either emotion categories or AUs. Experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in generating captions that describe details of facial actions and emotions, as well as the inferential relationship between them, even when those emotions are not present in the training data. It is also demonstrated that the vision-to-language task enhances the performance of the intermediate visual features on both AU detection and emotion classification tasks.

</details>


### [89] [Fault-Guided Cross-Layer Prediction for Unsupervised Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08140)
*Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 通过学习冻结视觉Transformer不同层之间的特征传递，并结合训练数据校准的前景先验，实现单类工业异常检测与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单类异常检测依赖正常样本特征重建；作者关注Transformer深层语义保留但局部细节丢失的问题，利用层间无法传递的信息识别异常。

Method: 将冻结视觉Transformer的深度视为离散状态转换，逐token学习有向层间隐状态预测；根据相邻层余弦相似度识别深度分段及敏感目标层，确定源层与目标层。采用仅由训练图像校准的有界前景先验，抑制背景证据，同时保留前景估计之外的缺陷证据。

Result: 图像级AUROC：MVTec-AD 99.75%、VisA 99.42%、Real-IAD 94.27%；Real-IAD对象级AUROC为96.26%。在256×256分辨率下，三个数据集的像素级P-AUROC/P-AUPRO分别为98.46%/95.97%、99.21%/96.12%和99.26%/96.59%。

Conclusion: 层间信息传递差异可用于单类异常检测与定位；源目标选择规则可迁移至ViT-B和RADIO-B。摘要未提供基线对比或消融结果，无法据此判断相对提升及各组件贡献。

Abstract: Feature reconstruction is widely used for unsupervised industrial anomaly detection in the single-class setting, where only normal images from one product category are available for training. We propose a single-class detector that asks what information one block can pass to another. We treat the depth of a frozen vision transformer as a sequence of discrete transitions. A directed predictor learns a block-to-block hidden-state transition pointwise over tokens. Adjacent-layer cosine similarity exposes four sharp fault lines in DINOv2 ViT-L, separating stable depth segments. The deepest blocks retain semantic context but lose local detail; a prediction from deep or shallow sources to sensitive targets therefore exposes the information that cannot be transported. The fault structure fixes the source and target roles, and the same rule transfers to ViT-B and RADIO-B. We use a bounded foreground prior calibrated solely on training images: it attenuates background evidence without also erasing defects outside the estimated foreground. Using one predictor architecture and a common training-only calibration protocol, image AUROC is 99.75 on MVTec-AD, 99.42 on VisA and 94.27 on Real-IAD. On Real-IAD the object-level AUROC is 96.26. At 256x256 pixel resolution, P-AUROC/P-AUPRO are 98.46/95.97 on MVTec-AD, 99.21/96.12 on VisA and 99.26/96.59 on Real-IAD.

</details>


### [90] [MCUFlowNet: Hardware-Aware Neural Architecture Search for Optical Flow on Microcontrollers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08147)
*Enmin Lin,Jona Beysens*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACE-NAS通过宏观架构固定峰值SRAM、微观搜索优化精度与延迟，为MCU生成高效稠密光流网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 搭载神经加速器的微控制器受片上SRAM限制，可处理的输入分辨率有限，而面向此类设备的稠密光流网络较少。

Method: 宏观设计固定主干及峰值SRAM，将全分辨率输出头的转置卷积替换为双线性上采样与卷积，并加入轻量级上下文模块；微观搜索算子和网络深度，权衡精度与编译器估计的延迟。

Result: 峰值SRAM降低18%；MCUFlowNet-S较重新训练的EdgeFlowNet快1.76倍，端点误差高1.6%；在2 m/s模拟森林飞行中，无碰撞到达率为51.5%，对照为41.8%。语义分割中的PIDNet-S搜索变体在相近精度下快1.17倍。

Conclusion: 将峰值内存控制与延迟搜索解耦，可高效设计适用于MCU的光流网络；该方法也展现出向语义分割迁移的潜力。

Abstract: Dense optical flow allows small drones to avoid obstacles. However, few dense optical flow networks are designed for microcontrollers (MCUs) with neural accelerators, whose on-chip SRAM limits the input resolution. We present PACE-NAS, a macro-to-micro neural architecture search framework that decouples memory from latency: a macro design fixes the backbone and its peak SRAM, and a micro design searches operators and depths to trade accuracy against compiler-estimated latency. We apply it to EdgeFlowNet on a Grove Vision AI V2 board (Ethos-U55), where profiling locates the peak SRAM at the full-resolution output head. Replacing transposed convolutions with bilinear upsampling and convolutions lowers the peak SRAM by 18%, and two lightweight context modules lower the endpoint error (EPE) without raising the peak. All searched subnets share this peak, so the search needs no memory constraint. We name the Pareto-optimal models MCUFlowNet; the fastest variant, MCUFlowNet-S, runs 1.76x faster than a retrained EdgeFlowNet at a 1.6% higher EPE and, in simulated forest flights at 2 m/s, reaches the goal without collision in 51.5% of trials, against 41.8%. PACE-NAS also applies to semantic segmentation, where a searched PIDNet-S variant runs 1.17x faster at similar accuracy. Code is available at https://gitlab.kuleuven.be/emedia-research-lab/esat-stadius/mcuflownet/mcuflownet.

</details>


### [91] [Improving Context-Shift Robustness of Convolutional Networks via Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08157)
*Jinen Setpal,Chaoyue Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出基于特征归因的训练框架，减少图像分类模型对背景上下文的依赖，提升上下文变化下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练与测试时的对象上下文可能显著不同；模型依赖非因果上下文线索会导致性能下降，现有特征归因方法还存在理论局限。

Method: 提出更忠实反映模型预测的归因方法 ContrastiveCAMs，并据此设计上下文正则化交叉熵损失（CR-CE），抑制上下文区域的影响，引导模型关注对象相关特征。

Result: 在 Waterbirds、Spawrious、ImageNet/ImageNet-BG 和 Hard-ImageNet 等数据集上，上下文偏移鲁棒性均得到一致改善；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 利用更忠实的空间特征归因约束训练，可降低模型对上下文的依赖，提高其应对上下文偏移的能力。

Abstract: Image classification models often encounter objects in diverse and unpredictable contexts at test time, which may differ substantially from those seen during training. This phenomenon, termed context shift, exposes a key fragility in neural networks: when models rely on non-causal contextual cues for prediction, shifts in context can lead to significant performance degradation. In this paper, we propose a novel training framework to improve the robustness of convolutional neural networks under context shift. Our approach leverages spatial feature attribution to guide models toward making predictions that rely less on contextual regions and more on object-relevant features. As a first step, recognizing a theoretical limitation of existing feature attribution methods we introduce a new variant, ContrastiveCAMs, which produces more faithful attribution maps of model predictions. Building on ContrastiveCAMs, we further propose Context-Regularized Cross-Entropy (CR-CE), a modification of the standard cross-entropy loss that regularizes the model's attention by suppressing the influence of contextual regions, thereby improving robustness to context shift. We evaluate the effectiveness of our approach on several medium-to-large scale datasets (Waterbirds, Spawrious, ImageNet/ImageNet-BG, Hard-ImageNet), and report consistent improvements in context-shift robustness.

</details>


### [92] [Multimodal Hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts for 3D Medical Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08185)
*Gizem Mert,Szymon Płotka,Pedro R. A. S. Bassi,Wenxuan Li,Zongwei Zhou,Jarosław B. Ćwikła,Arkadiusz Sitek,Ewa Szczurek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedHiVE通过分层软混合专家和多尺度对比学习，联合建模3D医学影像与报告中的局部解剖信息和全局上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学视觉语言模型常忽略影像与报告的层次结构，难以聚焦具有临床意义的解剖区域。

Method: 采用两级可学习令牌路由：局部SMoE处理影像和文本特征，全局SMoE融合多模态表示；结合报告导出的解剖级与全局视觉语言对比对齐。

Result: 在超过25,000例CT扫描上预训练后，该方法在6个数据集的分割、分类和检测任务中，表现优于11种先进方法；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 跨解剖尺度的动态多模态路由与对比对齐有助于提升3D医学影像任务表现。

Abstract: Medical 3D scans and reports are inherently hierarchical, with diagnostic cues concentrated in specific anatomical regions across multiple levels of organization. Yet, most medical vision-language models ignore this structure and fail to focus on clinically relevant anatomy. We introduce MedHiVE, which combines hierarchical vision-language Soft Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) with contrastive learning to jointly model fine-grained anatomy and global scan context. To our knowledge, MedHiVE is the first framework to route both vision and text tokens through a two-level SMoE hierarchy organized by anatomical scale, coupling this with report-derived anatomy-level contrastive alignment for 3D medical imaging. This replaces the direct region-text alignment of statically pooled features and vision-only hierarchical routing of prior work with dynamic, slot-based cross-modal information flow across anatomical scales. MedHiVE operates via a two-level, learnable token-routing mechanism, where local image and text features are first routed through a local SMoE and then fused by a global SMoE into unified multimodal representations. Contrastive learning aligns both anatomy-level and global vision-language representations in a unified space. Pre-trained on 25,000+ CT scans with anatomical supervision, MedHiVE consistently surpasses 11 state-of-the-art methods across 6 datasets on 3 tasks: segmentation, classification, and detection, demonstrating the importance of hierarchical modeling. The code for MedHiVE is at github.com/szczurek-lab/MedHiVE.

</details>


### [93] [SplatAnything: Gaussian Spacing Normalization for Feed-forward 3DGS across Arbitrary Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08264)
*Hao Yu,Zhenwei Wang,Yongzhen Hu,Yuning Gong,Junta Wu,Xiaowei Zhou,Hujun Bao,Tengfei Wang,Chunchao Guo,Sida Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SplatAnything通过高斯间距归一化缓解前馈式3D高斯泼溅中的监督歧义，并在多样场景和视角条件下取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常归一化场景的全局径向距离统计量，但相邻高斯中心的间距仍随场景尺度和相机配置显著变化，造成高斯尺度监督目标的系统性偏移和歧义。

Method: 提出高斯间距归一化（GSNorm），对场景级高斯间距的稳健统计量进行规范化，为高斯属性预测提供更一致的空间参照；同时建立涵盖多数据集、不同输入稀疏度及外推视角的基准。

Result: 摘要报告该方法达到当前最先进水平，在场景尺度和视角变化较大的条件下提升尤为明显；未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 规范化高斯间距有助于改善预测监督的一致性，并提升前馈式3D高斯泼溅在复杂尺度和视角变化下的表现。

Abstract: We identify a key limitation of existing feed-forward 3DGS methods: Gaussian attributes are typically predicted in a radial-distance-normalized space that canonicalizes a global radial statistic of the reconstructed geometry, while the spacing between neighboring Gaussian centers can still vary substantially across scene scales and camera configurations. This mismatch induces systematic shifts in Gaussian-scale targets, leading to ambiguous supervision. To address this, we propose SplatAnything with Gaussian Spacing Normalization (GSNorm), which canonicalizes a robust scene-level statistic of Gaussian spacing to provide a more consistent spatial reference for Gaussian prediction. To rigorously assess our method, we further establish a systematic benchmark spanning diverse datasets, input sparsity levels, and challenging extrapolated viewpoints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SplatAnything achieves state-of-the-art performance, with particularly strong gains under substantial variations in scene scale and viewpoint.

</details>


### [94] [DART: Domain-Agnostic Residual Transfer for Generalist Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08271)
*Muhammad Aqeel,Maham Nazir,Marco Cristani,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DART通过解耦异常线索与域特定信息，提升通用异常检测在未见域上的迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有正常—异常引导方法的残差混合了异常信息与纹理、几何及采集条件等域特征，限制跨域泛化并可能产生虚假激活。

Method: 提出架构无关的即插即用框架：残差解耦模块保留异常判别信息并抑制域信息；跨域元训练通过源域间任务促进有效解耦。该框架插入残差计算与检测头之间。

Result: 在MVTec AD、VisA和BraTS上，相对于多个基础方法均取得一致提升，域偏移越强时增益越明显；摘要未提供具体数值。

Conclusion: 残差解耦结合跨域元训练有助于改善通用异常检测的跨域迁移，且可适配不同基础架构。

Abstract: Generalist Anomaly Detection (GAD) aims to detect anomalies in unseen domains using only a few reference samples. Recent normal-abnormal guided methods improve this setting by exploiting abnormal references, but their residuals often mix transferable anomaly cues with domain-specific appearance factors such as texture, geometry, and acquisition conditions. This residual domain entanglement limits cross-domain transfer and may produce spurious activations on novel domains. We propose DART, a plug-in framework for normal-abnormal guided GAD that learns to separate residuals into anomaly-relevant and domain-specific components. DART combines a Residual Disentanglement Module, which preserves anomaly-discriminative information while suppressing domain information, with Cross-Domain Meta-Training, which forms episodes across source domains to make the separation effective. The method is architecture-agnostic and can be inserted between residual computation and the detection head of existing GAD pipelines. Experiments on MVTec AD, VisA, and BraTS show consistent improvements over multiple base methods, with the largest gains under stronger domain shifts. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/DART

</details>


### [95] [Interactive Visual Gym: Evaluating Visual Interaction Models with Simulated Users in Interactive Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08286)
*Chenhao Zheng,Zixian Ma,Kai Li,Kanchana Ranasinghe,Jieyu Zhang,Artemis Panagopoulou,Weikai Huang,Jianxu Shangguan,Yuanyuan Tan,Silvio Savarese,Juan Carlos Niebles,Ranjay Krishna,Ran Xu,Honglu Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出交互式视觉评测与训练框架，在闭环模拟中发现当前视觉语言模型完成任务和及时纠错的能力不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线回放无法反映助手回复对用户后续行为及任务结果的影响，因此需要闭环评估。

Method: 构建 Interactive Visual Gym，联合模拟可执行任务环境和基于用户画像的用户行为，涵盖烹饪、家务操作和移动界面；用户模拟包含受控执行错误及偏离计划的行为。基于该框架建立包含315个交互场景的 IVG-Bench，并开展人类验证与 GRPO 闭环训练实验。

Result: 表现最佳的 Gemini 4 Argon 在时限内完成53.4%的任务，事实依据和可操作指导能力存在明显不足，考虑响应延迟后也常无法及时防错。交互质量指标与参与者偏好的一致率为78.5%。概念验证实验中，GRPO 闭环训练提升了 Qwen3.5-4B 在烹饪环境中的任务成功率和交互质量。

Conclusion: 闭环仿真能够揭示离线评测难以捕捉的交互缺陷，并可为模型训练提供有用信号；训练效果目前仅在单一模型和烹饪环境中得到概念验证。

Abstract: Visual interaction models that support users during ongoing physical tasks require evaluation in closed loop, where assistant responses can change subsequent user behavior and task outcomes. Existing benchmarks largely rely on offline replay and therefore cannot capture these effects. We introduce Interactive Visual Gym, a framework for closed-loop evaluation and training of visual interaction models through joint simulation of executable task environments and human users. We contribute three simulators that recreate everyday tasks and human-aligned action spaces across cooking, household manipulation, and mobile-interface domains, together with a persona-conditioned user simulation harness that collaborates with the assistant while incorporating controlled execution errors and other out-of-plan behaviors. We instantiate the framework as IVG-Bench, a benchmark with 315 interaction scenarios. Evaluating state-of-the-art VLMs as visual interaction models reveals substantial limitations: the best-performing model, Gemini 4 Argon, completes only 53.4% of tasks within the time limit, exhibits significant weaknesses in factual grounding and actionable guidance, and is often too slow to prevent errors when response latency is considered. We verify our evaluation protocol with a human study which finds 78.5% agreement between our interaction-quality metric and participant preferences. We further show that the environments provide useful learning signals: in a proof-of-concept GRPO experiment, closed-loop training improves both task success and interaction quality for Qwen3.5-4B model in our cooking environment.

</details>


### [96] [Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting for Online Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08288)
*Linus Franke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HIBS 将 Beta 核引入在线增量式场景重建，在提升重建质量的同时降低优化时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线 3D Gaussian Splatting 每个关键帧的初始化和优化机会有限，难以修正初始误差与细节缺失；高斯核的柔软特性还需要大量重叠才能形成实体表面。

Method: 使用形状参数可调的 Beta 核表示几何置信度；依据几何可观测性与多视角深度一致性初始化不透明度和形状；将陈旧基元重定位至光度误差热点；利用有界核支持进行精确图块剔除，并通过保持形状的闭式合并构建细节层次。

Result: 在 Mip-NeRF 360 的即时重建协议下，PSNR 最多提高 0.8 dB，优化时间最多减少 40%。

Conclusion: 置信度感知的 Beta 核与增量密化、层次化表示相结合，可改善在线重建质量与效率，并支持实时渲染。

Abstract: Online 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructs a scene during capture: each keyframe spawns primitives once and refines them for only a few dozen iterations, leaving little chance to fix poor initialization or missing detail. Gaussians are also inherently soft, needing many overlaps for solid surfaces. We present Hierarchical Incremental Beta Splatting (HIBS), which brings beta kernels to incremental reconstruction. Their shape parameter interpolates between a soft, Gaussian-like profile and a flat-topped, near-opaque surfel, acting as a confidence dial for geometry. Our confidence-aware spawning uses geometric observability and multi-view depth agreement to initialize both opacity and shape, producing solid primitives where geometry is verifiable and soft, correctable primitives where it is not. As online densification, error-driven relocation recycles stale primitives into photometric-error hotspots. Bounded beta kernel support enables exact tile culling and faster rasterization, and a closed-form, shape-preserving merging formulation provides a level-of-detail (LOD) hierarchy for fast optimization and real-time rendering. On Mip-NeRF 360 in the on-the-fly protocol, HIBS improves PSNR by up to 0.8 dB while reducing optimization time by up to 40%. Project page: https://linus-franke.com/hibs/

</details>


### [97] [ManimCode: Learning Visual Self-Correction for Educational Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08294)
*Zhengyang Liang,Lizi Liao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ManimData 与 ManimCode 通过渲染反馈轨迹和强化学习，使单个 4B 模型更可靠地生成、审查和修正教育动画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渲染成功不等于动画正确或具有教学质量；现有智能体框架常依赖大型专有模型，而现有数据集缺少质量验证及反馈修正轨迹。

Method: 构建包含 24,034 条经渲染验证和质量过滤记录的 ManimData，保留中间程序、执行错误、视觉评论及修正；对单个 4B 模型进行角色条件监督微调，并在自生成状态上利用渲染结果和无参考质量反馈强化训练修正角色。

Result: 在 ManimData-test 和 ManimBench 上，渲染成功率分别达到 96.6% 和 97.0%，未训练 4B 基线为 52.6% 和 82.0%；视频质量分数分别由 2.48 升至 2.94、3.00 升至 3.44。两个长视频系统的完成数量分别由 19/117 升至 107/117、53/239 升至 239/239。

Conclusion: 反馈轨迹训练与基于自生成状态的强化学习可提高小模型的动画生成和修正能力，并改善长视频生成效果；摘要未提供质量评分尺度或消融结果。

Abstract: Manim supports precise, programmatic video generation, but a successfully rendered educational animation can still have visual defects, missing explanations, or incorrect content. Rendering does not directly identify the code changes needed to correct these visual failures, so feedback-driven revision is needed. Existing agentic frameworks use iterative rendering and revision but typically rely on large models, often proprietary. Existing Manim datasets primarily contain final programs and often lack quality verification and the trajectories of feedback and revisions needed to learn this refinement process. We introduce ManimData and ManimCode, a data and training framework for feedback-driven educational animation generation. We construct ManimData through a render-guided pipeline that iteratively generates, executes, reviews, and revises Manim programs with retrieved API documentation. Its 24,034 render-verified, quality-filtered records preserve intermediate programs, execution errors, visual critiques, and corrective revisions. We use role-conditioned supervised fine-tuning on these trajectories to train ManimCode, a single 4B model, to write, review, and revise animation programs. To improve correction of errors that differ from those in the collected trajectories, we then train the Reviser with reinforcement learning on self-generated states, using rendering outcomes and reference-free quality feedback. ManimCode achieves 96.6% and 97.0% render success on ManimData-test and ManimBench, compared with 52.6% and 82.0% for the untrained 4B model under the same inference setup, while also improving video quality scores from 2.48 to 2.94 and from 3.00 to 3.44. Integrating ManimCode into two long-form educational video generation systems increases completed videos from 19/117 to 107/117 and from 53/239 to 239/239, alongside improvements in educational effectiveness and visual quality.

</details>


### [98] [InstantHand: Feed-Forward 3D Gaussian Splatting for Animatable Hand Avatar Reconstruction from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08330)
*Chanho Kim,Muhammad Salman Ali,Kyeonghwan Gwak,Muhammad Shaheryar,Incheol Park,Jeongwan On,Seungryul Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstantHand 从单张图像快速构建可动画的三维高斯手部头像，无需测试时优化，同时兼顾轮廓与外观保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有手部头像重建依赖多视角采集或逐主体优化，扩展性受限；图像平均损失难以约束透明度泄漏，外观回归也可能偏离观测颜色。

Method: 基于现成的 MANO、相机和掩膜估计，通过前馈网络构建三维高斯手部头像；采用软 Alpha 边界带正则化强化轮廓监督，并根据高斯元的渲染贡献进行可靠性门控外观迁移，对未观测区域使用以全局源身份为条件的先验补全。

Result: 头像构建耗时 2.84 秒，渲染速度为 161 FPS；在 InterHand2.6M 上达到逐主体优化基线的水平，在 HanCo 零样本评估中保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，单图前馈重建可在避免测试时优化的同时实现快速、可动画的手部头像；摘要未提供具体误差指标或消融结果。

Abstract: Hand avatar reconstruction typically relies on multi-view capture or per-subject optimization, limiting its scalability. We introduce InstantHand, a feed-forward framework that constructs an animatable 3D Gaussian hand avatar from a single image using off-the-shelf MANO, camera, and mask estimates, without test-time optimization. Image-averaged losses provide insufficient supervision for alpha leakage, which thickens thin finger contours. To address this issue, we propose a Soft Alpha Band regularizer that concentrates supervision on silhouette boundaries. Furthermore, colors regressed from image features can drift toward a learned prior even in observed regions. Our Reliability-gated Appearance Transfer preserves observed appearance by transferring source colors according to each Gaussian's rendering contribution, while completing unobserved regions using the learned prior conditioned on global source identity. InstantHand constructs an avatar in 2.84 seconds and renders at 161 FPS, matching per-subject-optimized baselines on InterHand2.6M and remaining competitive in zero-shot evaluation on HanCo.

</details>


### [99] [4D Point Splatting for mmWave Radar Micro-Doppler Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08342)
*Adnan Armouti,Guanyue Qian,Rajalakshmi Nandakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 4D Point Splatting（4DPS）通过物理可微渲染拟合真实毫米波雷达动态场景，生成原始复数回波，显著缩小合成数据到真实数据的域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实雷达数据集规模远落后于视觉和语言领域；现有正向模拟、生成模型及静态场景逆渲染方法无法拟合真实人体运动采集数据，导致仿真到真实的差距。

Method: 利用同步相机初始化房间和人体，以携带材质、法线和速度的定向点表示场景，将点投影至距离和多普勒单元，生成原始复数回波；再通过常规信号处理获得下游模型所需表示，并采用自定义CUDA核加速拟合。

Result: 在HuPR和RT-Pose分别拟合235和100个序列；距离-方位相关性中位数为0.940和0.830，距离-多普勒为0.829和0.695，微多普勒为0.988和0.966。RT-Pose上，合成数据训练的姿态模型在真实数据上MPJPE为21.9 cm，真实数据训练为16.7 cm，RF-Genesis基线为127.6 cm。单张H100拟合每个序列分别需5.7和12.9分钟。

Conclusion: 该方法支持数据集规模的毫米波雷达逆渲染，并有望用于人体感知任务的合成数据生成及场景编辑增强；但合成训练与真实训练仍存在性能差距。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave radar resolves the micrometer displacements of a moving body as micro-Doppler, enabling gait, gesture, activity, pose and vital-sign sensing. Each task relies on a downstream model trained on real data, yet radar datasets lag orders of magnitude behind vision and language. Brute-force collection will not close that gap, and existing synthesis methods (forward-only simulators, learned generators and static-scene inverse renderers) cannot fit a real capture of a moving body, leaving a sim2real gap. We present 4D Point Splatting (4DPS), a physics-based differentiable renderer that fits an explicit moving scene to a real capture. Using the synchronized camera to initialize the room and body, we represent the scene as oriented points carrying material, normal and velocity, and splat each point into range and Doppler bins. Our output is the raw complex return a real radar records, so we apply the same signal processing on the rendered data to obtain any representation for training any SOTA downstream model. We fit 235 sequences on HuPR and 100 on RT-Pose, two datasets with different radars. Our reconstructions set the state of the art, with median correlations of 0.940 and 0.830 on range-azimuth, 0.829 and 0.695 on range-Doppler, and 0.988 and 0.966 on micro-Doppler. Our renders also set the state of the art in downstream sim2real gap. On RT-Pose, a pose model trained on them reaches 21.9 cm MPJPE on real data, against 16.7 cm for real training and 127.6 cm for the RF-Genesis baseline, where both the rendered- and real-trained models learn nearly the same features. Our custom CUDA kernels fit a sequence in 5.7 minutes on HuPR and 12.9 on RT-Pose on one H100, making dataset-scale inverse rendering of mmWave radar possible for the first time. This work can enable synthetic data generation for any human-centered downstream task and model, and scene editing for data augmentation.

</details>


### [100] [USB Gym: A Gymnasium Testbed for Closed-Loop Ultrasound View Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08352)
*Thanapong Intharah,Zhichao Gao,Yiliang Liu,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究提出超声探头引导测试平台 USB Gym；组合现有方法后，探头距目标仍有 13.7 mm，远逊于仿真中的位姿预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引导组件通常在不同模拟器中分别评估，缺少统一环境来检验组合后的实际引导效果。

Method: 基于公开患者 CT 和器官标签生成仿真超声图像；在器官分割、控制器和奖励函数三个可替换模块中评估 20 种已发表方法，并以仿真专用位姿预言机作为参考。

Result: 在 20 例新 CT 上，探头初始平均距离为 16.0 mm，位姿预言机达到 1.5 mm；仅 SSIM 图像相似度奖励显示出可检测的小幅改进，组合基线最终为 13.7 mm，完成预言机进展的约 16%。

Conclusion: 未经针对性调优的现有方法组合未能使探头接近目标视图；结果属探索性，且未在真实硬件上验证。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Our main goal is ultrasound guidance: moving a probe until its image matches a target view. Published methods for each part of a guide are evaluated in separate simulators. We asked whether these methods, combined into one guide, are good enough.
  Methods: We built USB Gym, a testbed whose standard engine simulates ultrasound from public patient CT, with a network drawing organ textures from CT labels. Its guide has three replaceable parts (slots), an organ segmenter, a controller and a reward, which received 20 published methods, each run on the same 60 episodes of six held-out patients. In each slot, a published method replaced ours only if detectably better on these patients. The better of this combination and our original guide on 20 new CTs is the benchmark baseline. A pose oracle, reading the exact probe pose available only in simulation, set the reference. The results below are exploratory.
  Results: On these CTs, the probe started on average 16.0 mm from the target view; the pose oracle brought it to 1.5 mm. Of the results known when the combination was fixed, only an image-similarity reward (SSIM) was slightly but detectably better, so the combination was our guide with that reward. This combination was the benchmark baseline, ending at 13.7 mm (16% of the oracle's progress).
  Conclusions: Combining untuned published methods with ours did not guide the probe near the target view; the task remains under-explored. The testbed accepts new methods; no guide was tested on hardware.

</details>


### [101] [OraRefer: Segmentation-Guided Multi-Photograph Fusion for Oral Cancer Screening with Guaranteed Patient-Level Sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08360)
*Rupam Mukherjee,Mehakdeep Singh,Shirin Dasgupta,Subhamoy Mandal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OraRefer融合患者多张口腔照片和风险因素，在患者层面进行转诊筛查，并通过校准阈值提供有限样本敏感度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法主要分类单张照片，但临床转诊针对患者，通常需要综合多张照片，并控制漏诊比例。

Method: 采用分割引导的基础模型，根据预测病灶对每张照片分类；融合患者的所有照片及风险因素；利用有限样本校准设定转诊阈值。模型自动定位口腔，推理时无需人工标注。

Result: 在714名患者的3000张照片上，患者级融合的AUROC为0.919，优于基于注意力的多实例学习。以90%敏感度为目标时，检出95%的口腔潜在恶性疾病或口腔癌患者；61名癌症患者中漏诊3人，特异度为0.610。

Conclusion: 患者级多图像与风险因素融合有望改善口腔筛查；无需重新训练即可利用本地校准样本调整转诊阈值。不过，摘要未说明外部验证及敏感度保证的具体统计条件。

Abstract: Oral cancer and the oral potentially malignant disorders (OPMD) that can precede it are visible on the oral mucosa, which makes smartphone photographs a promising tool for screening. Deep learning models for this task classify individual photographs. However, screening refers patients, not photographs: a patient is usually photographed several times, and the referral decision must miss no more than a set share of patients with disease. We propose OraRefer, a patient-level screening model that reads each photograph with a segmentation-guided foundation model that conditions its class decision on the predicted lesion, combines all photographs of a patient with risk factors, and refers through a threshold with a finite-sample sensitivity guarantee. The model localizes the mouth itself, so inference needs no annotation. On 3000 photographs of 714 patients, patient-level fusion reaches an AUROC of 0.919 and outperforms attention-based multiple-instance learning. At a 90% sensitivity target, OraRefer detects 95% of patients with OPMD or cancer and misses 3 of 61 patients with cancer, at a specificity of 0.610. Because the threshold is calibrated without retraining, each clinic can set its own guaranteed operating point on a local calibration sample.

</details>


### [102] [Adaptive Low-Light Enhancement for Road Anomaly Detection: A Multi-Architecture Benchmark and Edge Deployment Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08393)
*Obed Honour Eje,Oluwatosin Kolade,Glory Bagai,Taliha Abiodun Folorunso*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对恶劣视觉条件下的道路异常检测，论文提出无需训练的亮度自适应增强路由，以减少固定增强策略的性能损失，并在边缘设备上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶道路异常检测需要应对黑暗、雨天和沙尘霾；统一使用低光增强可能在黄昏等条件下损害检测性能。

Method: 比较YOLOv8n、YOLOv12n、RT-DETR和Faster R-CNN在符合物理规律的光照及天气退化条件下的表现；根据逐帧Rec. 601亮度，在不增强、中等强度SCI增强、去噪加高强度SCI增强之间选择，并在Jetson Nano上验证完整流程。

Result: 统一使用SCI在黄昏级光照下使mAP@50下降16.1%至30.7%；自适应路由恢复了大部分性能损失，同时保留低光条件下的增益。摘要未给出路由后的具体数值或设备运行速度。

Conclusion: 按亮度动态选择预处理强度比统一增强更稳健，且该轻量、免训练方案已在边缘硬件上完成验证；具体实时性和泛化能力仍需结合正文判断。

Abstract: Road anomaly detection systems on autonomous vehicles must function reliably under degraded visual conditions including darkness, rain, and Harmattan dust haze. This paper benchmarks four object detection architectures, YOLOv8n, YOLOv12n, RT-DETR, and Faster R-CNN, under physically grounded lighting and weather degradation, and shows that uniform application of the SCI low-light enhancement network is actively harmful at dusk-level lighting (-16.1% to -30.7% mAP@50). We propose a lightweight, training-free luminance-adaptive router that selects between no enhancement, moderate SCI, and denoising plus strong SCI based on per-frame Rec. 601 luminance, recovering most of the dusk penalty while preserving low-light gains, and we validate the full pipeline on NVIDIA Jetson Nano edge hardware.

</details>


### [103] [SM4RT: Cascaded Feed-forward Model for 4D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08436)
*Pang-Chi Lo,Jhao-Syun Lai,Tsung-Wei Ke*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SM4RT 通过级联三维几何重建与运动估计，从 RGB 视频重建动态场景，并利用弱监督缓解运动标签稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈方法通常将几何重建与运动估计分开处理；在单一坐标系中预测运动还需同时考虑相机运动，容易产生歧义。此外，真实场景的运动标签稀缺。

Method: 先重建三维几何，再利用预测结构引导运动估计；在各像素对应的相机坐标系中表示其三维运动；通过弱监督损失，利用另一时间步的已标注点位置约束未标注点的运动。

Result: 在 Kubric、Waymo 和 Point Odyssey 三个动态场景数据集上，运动估计在多数指标上优于既有基线；在五个动态场景数据集上的几何重建表现与最先进方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 级联整合几何重建与运动估计可避免两项任务间的性能权衡，并通过更好的几何推理提升运动估计。

Abstract: We introduce SM4RT, a multi-task cascade feed-forward model for dynamic scene reconstruction from RGB videos. While existing feed-forward approaches typically address geometry reconstruction and motion estimation separately--and often predict motions in a single coordinate frame that must account for camera movement--SM4RT solves these tasks sequentially by first reconstructing 3D geometry and then bootstrapping motion estimation from the predicted structure. By expressing each pixel's 3D motion in its corresponding camera frame, SM4RT reduces ambiguity arising from camera motion. To address the sparsity of real-world motion labels, SM4RT further incorporates a weakly supervised loss that regularizes unlabeled point motions using labeled point positions at the other time step. We evaluate motion estimation on three dynamic-scene datasets--Kubric, Waymo, and Point Odyssey--and SM4RT achieves the best performance on most metrics compared with prior baselines. We additionally benchmark geometry reconstruction on five dynamic scene datasets and show that SM4RT achieves competitive performance with state-of-the-art methods. Overall, our unified framework demonstrates that combining geometry reconstruction and motion estimation in a cascade not only avoids performance trade-offs but also enhances motion estimation through improved geometric reasoning.

</details>


### [104] [Edge-Assisted Crowdsensing for Explainable Cyclist Safety Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08441)
*Hongsen Cao,Chia-Yen Chiang,Mona Jaber,Meihui Wang,Yasmin Fathy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出边缘辅助众包系统，融合视觉语言模型、道路信息与骑行运动数据，以可解释模型预测骑行感知安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事故统计和多模型检测流程难以扩展为沿路线连续监测感知安全；自动驾驶车辆使混合交通更加复杂。

Method: 车把摄像头采集街景帧，视觉语言模型通过单次提示提取结构化交通属性；可解释提升机融合这些属性、OpenStreetMap道路设施及骑行运动学数据，预测众包1—9分安全评分。

Result: 两条伦敦路线共1,362帧的五折交叉验证中，二分类准确率为72.2%，平均绝对误差为1.011；准确率与黑箱集成模型相差不到1个百分点。最快自托管模型提取每帧耗时2.49秒，CPU评分耗时0.4毫秒。物理隔离、施划车道和施工场地的模型贡献分别为+0.97、+0.70和−0.65分。重编码后，20秒采样间隔可在54 Mbit/s下服务1,000名骑行者，准确率下降1.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 该方法兼顾预测性能、可解释性和扩展性，有望支持持续骑行安全监测及设施评估；但数据仅涵盖两条路线，标注者一致性中等偏低（α=0.4695），模型归因亦不等同于因果效应。

Abstract: Cyclist fatality rates remain high despite policy support for active travel, and autonomous vehicles add complexity to mixed traffic. Neither crash statistics nor multi-model detection pipelines scale to continuous monitoring of perceived safety along a route. We present an edge-assisted crowdsensing system: handlebar cameras supply street-level frames, a vision-language model (VLM) turns each frame into structured traffic attributes through a single prompt, and an explainable boosting machine (EBM) combines them with OpenStreetMap road infrastructure and on-bike kinematics to predict a crowdsourced 1-9 safety score. Extraction belongs at the network edge: the fastest self-hosted model needs 2.49 s per frame and scoring takes 0.4 ms on a CPU. On 1,362 annotated frames from two London routes, the EBM regressor reaches 72.2 percent binary accuracy and a mean absolute error of 1.011 under five-fold cross-validation. It is within one accuracy point of black-box ensembles. Its per-feature shape functions give exact attribution: physical lane separation is worth +0.97 rating points, a painted lane +0.70 and a construction site -0.65. Planners can therefore price an infrastructure change in rating points, and the results enter cost-benefit analysis directly. We also report inter-annotator agreement (alpha = 0.4695), an attribute audit, and a sensitivity analysis linking attribute error to accuracy. With re-encoding, a 20 s sampling interval serves 1,000 riders at 54 Mbit/s at a cost of 1.4 accuracy points, making continuous monitoring practical.

</details>


### [105] [EgoGeneralist: Unifying Egocentric Action-Video Tasks through Video-Native Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08477)
*Yanlin Jin,Bowen Yang,Cheng Zhang,Yifan Yang,Zhe Li,Jianfei Cai,Kai Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoGeneralist 将预训练视频生成模型适配为统一的动作—视频框架，支持度量三维人体运动控制、姿态估计及联合生成，并可泛化至未见场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频模型具有较强的视觉先验，但无法原生表示度量三维空间中的人体动作，因而难以统一动作控制、运动恢复与视频生成。

Method: 提出角色自适应动作渲染器，将度量运动转换为适合视频模型的表示，使其既可作为空间控制信号，也可作为待恢复的生成目标；通过轻量级跨流动作—视频耦合建立运动与视觉动态的对应关系，同时保留预训练视觉先验。

Result: 框架覆盖五项动作—视频任务。尽管仅在受控实验室录像上进行适配，模型仍能泛化至多样的未见场景和交互，表现出有效的动作控制与视觉质量；摘要未提供定量结果或具体任务清单。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，轻量级适配结合视频原生的动作表示，可连接预训练视觉知识与度量三维人体运动，构建跨任务的通用动作—视频框架。

Abstract: Modern video models possess strong visual priors, yet do not natively represent human action in metric 3D space. We present EgoGeneralist, which adapts pretrained video generators to unify action-conditioned egocentric video generation, pose estimation, and joint action-video generation. Our role-adaptive action renderer provides video-native representations for multidirectional action-video learning, making metric motion usable as both a spatial control signal and a recoverable generation target. Cross-stream action-video coupling establishes close correspondence between metric motion and visual dynamics through lightweight adaptation while preserving the pretrained visual prior. Together, these designs establish a generalist framework spanning five action-video tasks. Despite adaptation only on controlled laboratory recordings, EgoGeneralist generalizes to diverse unseen scenes and interactions with effective action control and visual quality, bridging pretrained visual knowledge and metric 3D human motion.

</details>


### [106] [Where and Why is Grounded Reasoning Useful?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08485)
*David Yan,Le Xue,Forrest Huang,Qiuyi Zhang,Dustin Tran,Andrew M. Dai,Yinfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 视觉坐标接地主要有助于计数和需要难以语言描述的视觉指代的任务；论文提出 BrickVision 衡量此类能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对坐标接地推理的实际收益、适用范围及原因缺乏系统分析。

Method: 提出思维链分析方法，衡量模型对生成坐标的使用程度；对接地推理训练方案开展消融，并通过合成任务研究视觉指代的可描述性。

Result: 接地推理提升了计数能力，但在广泛的空间推理和感知基准上，相比无坐标接地的基线没有显著收益；其在需要难以语言描述的视觉指代时最有用。

Conclusion: 许多现有基准可通过可描述的文本指代解决，难以充分衡量接地推理的价值；为此论文提出 BrickVision。

Abstract: Grounded reasoning, where a model outputs text coordinates of relevant locations in chain-of-thought reasoning, is a common strategy for solving complex visual reasoning problems. However, the benefits and limitations of grounded reasoning are understudied. We study where and why grounded reasoning is most useful. To do so, we introduce a chain-of-thought analysis method to measure how much a model uses its outputted grounding in its thinking trace and perform a detailed ablation study of a grounded reasoning training recipe. We find that grounded reasoning improves counting but shows no significant gain over baselines without coordinate grounding across a wide range of spatial reasoning and perception benchmarks. Using several synthetic tasks, we show that grounded reasoning is most useful when a task requires undescribable references, which are visual concepts that are difficult to describe in language. However, we observe that many existing visual reasoning benchmarks are solvable with only describable text references. To close this gap, we introduce BrickVision, a new benchmark that requires undescribable visual references to solve.

</details>


### [107] [Do You Miss the Nights We Spent Crafting Figures? Rethinking Teaser and Framework Figures in the Age of Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08486)
*Xiaoqi Zhao,Youwei Pang,Kailai Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究分析371幅科研图示及其AI修订版本：修订提高了清晰度，但略微降低了美学连贯性；作者应主导设计并负责科学表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨生成式AI如何影响科研图示的解释清晰度和有目的的视觉表达。

Method: 回顾200篇论文中的371幅动机图和框架图；样本包括跨四个时期的160篇配额主样本及40篇目的性风格对比样本。单一多模态评审从六个维度对每幅图重复评价三次，并对全部图示进行受源内容约束的修订；369幅通过记录的保真检查。

Result: 原图评分未随时期呈现一致下降。修订后，五分制平均清晰度提高0.428分，美学连贯性降低0.111分；主样本中，动机图的六维平均评分增益大于框架图。案例显示，增加解释可澄清比较，但文字层级和视觉简洁性仍可能存在问题。

Conclusion: 模型评估结果支持研究者主导、AI辅助探索的设计方式；作者仍需负责解释目的、构图和科学关系。结果受单一模型评审及样本设计限制。

Abstract: Scientific figures give research ideas a visual form through choices of abstraction, composition, and emphasis. Generative AI offers new ways to realize these choices, raising questions about explanatory clarity and purposeful visual expression. We examine these questions through a visual retrospective of 371 motivation and framework figures from 200 papers. The corpus comprises a quota-based main sample of 160 papers spanning four temporal periods and a separate purposive style-contrast sample of 40 papers. A single multimodal judge evaluates six dimensions of communication and design, with three repeated judgments per figure. We attempt source-constrained revisions of all 371 figures, of which 369 pass the recorded fidelity checks. Original-figure ratings show no uniform decline across periods. Revisions increase mean clarity ratings by 0.428 points and decrease aesthetic coherence ratings by 0.111 points on a five-point scale. Gains in the six-dimension average are larger for main-sample motivation figures than for frameworks. Case analyses show that added explanation can clarify comparisons while leaving typographic hierarchy and visual economy unresolved. These model-based findings support researcher-led design, with AI assisting exploration and authors retaining responsibility for explanatory purpose, composition, and scientific relationships. The accompanying atlas and evaluation records are available at https://ntu-ai4x.github.io/Rethinking_Figures/index_en.html.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Interactive Backdoor Attacks in LLM-Based Proactive Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06969)
*Zhiyang Xia,Yuming Shang,Xi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出通过微调数据投毒植入交互式后门，使主动对话系统在触发后持续朝攻击者目标引导，同时基本维持正常效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型后门研究主要关注触发后的单次指定回复，尚未充分研究目标导向的多轮交互操纵。

Method: 通过投毒微调对话植入触发条件下的引导策略偏差，设计直接攻击、受约束渐进攻击和非受约束渐进攻击，并在心理咨询场景下评估。

Result: 在两个模型和两个对话数据集上，三种攻击在各自交互设置下均有效，正常效用大体保留；所测数据过滤器保留了几乎所有投毒对话，三种数据接纳后的防御基线仍存在显著攻击成功。

Conclusion: 微调数据投毒可造成持续的多轮对话引导风险；所测试的防御方法不足以有效消除此风险。摘要未提供具体量化结果。

Abstract: LLM-based proactive dialogue systems provide goal-directed support through sustained interaction. Fine-tuning their underlying models on externally supplied dialogue data introduces backdoor risks. Existing LLM backdoor studies primarily focus on eliciting an attacker-specified response upon trigger activation. We present an interactive backdoor attack framework that uses training-data poisoning to steer post-trigger, multi-turn interactions toward an attacker-specified objective. Poisoned fine-tuning dialogues implant a trigger-conditioned guidance-policy bias that ordinary user utterances can activate, without deployment-time prompt modification or further attacker intervention. Specifically, we instantiate three complementary attacks under progressively expanded post-trigger interaction settings: (1) Direct Attack, which targets an immediate response deviation upon trigger activation, retaining the conventional trigger--response setting; (2) Constrained Progressive Attack, which progressively advances the attack objective along a predefined multi-turn trajectory; and (3) Unconstrained Progressive Attack, which extends target-directed steering to open-ended interactions with varied user continuations. Using psychological counseling as a representative setting, experiments on two LLM backbones and two dialogue datasets show that all three attacks effectively achieve their objectives under their respective interaction settings while largely preserving normal utility. Separate defense evaluations show that the tested training-data filters retain nearly all poisoned dialogues, while three post-admission baselines leave substantial residual attack-specific success.

</details>


### [109] [On the Opportunities and Challenges of Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-Model Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06976)
*Zhenyi Shen,Junru Lu,Wei Liu,Jiangnan Ye,Jiazheng Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Tameem Adel,Yali Du,Lin Gui,Yulan He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出跨模型潜在通信框架 PLatour 和评估方法 PACT-Check，并发现性能提升不一定源于发送模型的有效信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大、小语言模型协作系统缺少独立评估手段；仅凭任务性能提升，无法证明接收模型利用了发送模型的信息。

Method: 通过轻量级、零初始化的线性桥接层连接冻结模型，直接利用任务奖励训练桥接层，并支持混合注意力架构；通过受控实验及 PACT-Check 衡量通信的真实贡献。

Result: PLatour 在生成任务上显著优于开源基线，并以更小的发送模型超过 Mostik 在共同数学和编程基准上的报告结果。但部分任务的收益也可通过无信息发送信号、自通信或仅训练接收模型复现。

Conclusion: 可训练桥接层可能只是充当参数高效适配机制，而未利用发送模型的有用信息；评估 PACT 系统时应区分适配收益与真正的跨模型通信收益。

Abstract: Prefill-to-Autoregressive Cross-model Transfer (PACT), a form of latent communication between language models, combines a larger model's prompt representations with a smaller model's efficient generation, offering a promising way to balance capability and inference cost. However, existing systems provide limited support for independently evaluating large-small model collaboration, and improved task performance alone does not establish whether the receiver benefits from the sender's information. We introduce PLatour, an open-source PACT framework that connects frozen models through lightweight, zero-initialized linear bridges trained directly with task rewards and supports hybrid-attention architectures. PLatour significantly outperforms open-source baselines on generation tasks and surpasses Mostik's reported results on shared mathematics and coding benchmarks using a substantially smaller sender. However, controlled experiments show that the gains on some tasks can be reproduced by uninformative sender signals, self-communication, or receiver-only training. We find that trainable bridges can act as parameter-efficient adaptation mechanisms without exploiting useful sender information. We therefore propose PACT-Check to quantify how much of a trained PACT system's gain genuinely comes from communication. Finally, we discuss the opportunities and challenges of PACT systems. Code is available at https://github.com/zhenyi4/platour.

</details>


### [110] [Follow the Policy, Fail the Benchmark: Auditing and Revising Policy-Based Decision Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07042)
*Yanru Xiao,Yifei Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文审计九个基于规则的决策基准，发现参考答案和评分器错误会显著扭曲模型评估，并提出可版本化的审计与修订资源 BAR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使模型正确遵循书面规则，基准中的错误目标、参考答案或评分逻辑也可能导致错误判分，降低评测结果的可信度。

Method: 通过追溯原始规则、执行程序及开展受控检查审计九个公开基准；在不改变模型回答的情况下重新评分，并设计控制中间答案错误的实验。

Result: 在 LegalBench SARA 的96个数值案例中，仅修正评分器便使三个模型各自的答案接受率提高超过80个百分点；在 RuleArena Tax 中恢复必要的零下限后，300个参考答案中有27个改变，且观察到的模型差距缩小。受控实验还表明，错误的中间答案可能影响最终决策。

Conclusion: 基于规则的基准需要系统审计参考答案、评分器和中间步骤；BAR 提供版本化审计证据与修订，支持重新评估。

Abstract: Policy-based decision benchmarks evaluate whether systems apply written rules to financial and operational cases. Yet a rule-following response can be marked wrong when the benchmark misstates the required decision. We audit nine public policy-based decision benchmarks through source tracing, program execution, and controlled checks. We find unsupported targets, reference errors that survive replay or propagate as solved steps, and evaluators that reject acceptable answers or credit incomplete decisions. To measure the scoring impact, we rescore unchanged model responses. On the 96 LegalBench SARA numeric cases, correcting the scorer raises acceptance by more than 80 percentage points for each of three models, without changing references or responses. In RuleArena Tax, restoring a required zero floor changes 27 of 300 references and narrows an observed model gap. A controlled federal-exemption task with explicit ownership and eligibility assumptions also shows that errors in supplied intermediate answers can affect final decisions. We present BAR (Benchmark Audit and Revision), a versioned collection of audit evidence and revisions for reevaluation.

</details>


### [111] [Reclassification Is All You Missed: Surfacing Variant Reinterpretation Need in Online Health Communities at Population Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07054)
*Kamalakkannan Ravi,Mengshu Nie,Annapurna Poduri,Cong Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PRISM利用智能体式大语言模型筛查在线健康社区中的遗传变异讨论，识别潜在分类变化并草拟提醒；试运行显示其具有发现价值，但外联消息的临床适用性仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ClinVar中的遗传变异可能在患者检测后被重新分类，但患者重新联系机制不一致、责任分散，且临床渠道难以覆盖部分在线社区用户。

Method: 构建智能体式大语言模型流程，筛查帖子、核查变异后续分类并生成含免责声明的外联草稿；开展不实际联系用户的试运行，并由临床医生评估30份真实帖子对应的草稿。

Result: 在32个社区的390万篇帖子中，预测与重新解读相关的讨论占0.288%；专业遗传学社区为6.30%，普通医疗建议社区为0.10%，相关讨论自2014年以来增长五倍。系统发现123个候选分类差异，其中5个从意义未明变为致病或可能致病。30份草稿中，27份被认为有事实依据，15份适合发送。

Conclusion: PRISM可用于大规模描绘遗传变异重新解读需求，但事实准确只是临床可接受外联的必要条件，并非充分条件；代码和数据计划在发表时发布。

Abstract: Genetic variants are routinely reclassified in ClinVar after a patient is tested, sometimes from uncertain to pathogenic, yet patient recontact is inconsistent, with no clear duty and responsibility diffused among labs, providers, and patients. Many affected people discuss their results in public online health communities that clinical recontact often does not reach. We built PRISM, an agentic large-language-model pipeline that screens these posts, checks whether the variant was later reclassified, and drafts a disclaimer-bearing outreach message, in a no-contact dry run. Across 3.9 million posts from 32 communities, agent-predicted reinterpretation-relevant discussion is rare (0.288%) but concentrated in specialist genetics communities (6.30% versus 0.10% in general medical-advice communities) and growing fivefold since 2014; PRISM surfaces 123 candidate classification differences, 5 of them from an uncertain submission to pathogenic or likely pathogenic. On a held-out set of 30 real-post drafts, a clinician judged 27 factually grounded and 15 fit to send: factual accuracy is necessary but not sufficient for clinically acceptable outreach. PRISM contributes a population-scale reinterpretation-need map with code and data to be released upon publication.

</details>


### [112] [Automated Feedback to Peer Tutors Increases Their Talk Moves and Learner Reasoning but Not the Balance of Talk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07072)
*Dorottya Demszky,Yann Hicke,Joy Yun,Mariah Olson,Max Karpawich,Anees Iqbal,Matt Wu,Jimmy Huang,Akshay Ravikumar,Drew Bent*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 面向导师的自动化反馈改善了引导学员思考的教学话语，但未减少导师发言时间；向学员额外提供反馈也未带来进一步改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明，课堂话语自动化反馈可改善教学；向学员提供反馈可能通过共同承担参与责任增强效果，但这一假设尚未在小组志愿辅导中得到检验。

Method: 在Schoolhouse.world平台两期各四周的SAT数学强化课程中开展随机对照试验，涉及1,030名志愿导师、7,443名学员和6,508次录制课程。导师被随机分配至无反馈、仅导师反馈，或导师反馈加学员参与反馈组；学员反馈采用两种激励框架。另访谈17名导师和13名学员。

Result: 导师反馈使引出学员观点、回应学员贡献及学员推理等指标提高8%—15%，且两期结果一致。对照组导师平均占据91.5%的发言时间，各条件均未改变这一比例；学员反馈也未进一步改变课堂话语。访谈显示，学员不回应限制了导师实践；学员反馈效果有限的可能原因包括反馈仅提供小组统计、部分学员偏好倾听，以及学员间互动较少。

Conclusion: 自动化反馈可以改善导师引导学员思考的方式，但增加学员发言量可能需要反馈之外的干预；访谈所示原因属于可能解释。

Abstract: Automated feedback on classroom discourse improves instruction in professional and online teaching contexts, and recent work suggests that delivering similar feedback to learners may strengthen these effects by sharing responsibility for participation. Whether this holds in small-group volunteer tutoring has not been tested.
